<!-- README.md -->

# ∅ nilware.io — founding framework (v2)

The founding documents of the nilware.io group: the operating company **nilware.io IKE**, the holding and fundraising vehicle **nilware.io Holdings AE**, the agreements between them, the constitution of the founder pool, and the policy for an agentic company.

> **Status: draft.** Every legal document is a draft for review by a Greek lawyer and accountant. It is not legal advice. Nothing is to be signed or filed until legal and accounting have signed off. Values still to be decided are in square brackets and listed in [OPEN_ITEMS.md](OPEN_ITEMS.md).

## Structure

```
charter/          the preface and the poems
articles/         articles of association (bylaws) of the IKE and the AE, statutes of the AMKE, constitution of the founder pool
agreements/       shareholders' agreement, Founding Person's IP assignment, brand licence
policies/         operating policies under the articles
filings/          instructions for registrations (trademarks)
correspondence/   cover letters for reviewers
```

## Ownership at a glance (from the IKE Articles, Arts. 4.1, 4.2)

The capital is 1,000 shares, so one share is 0.1 % and each Prozent's 1 % is 10 shares.

| Holder | Shares | Share |
|---|---|---|
| Founder (the founder pool, Art. 4.2E) | 500 founder-class shares (Class F), held at first by the Founding Person | 50 %, always exactly: ordinary voting shares voting as one block through the Founder Representative, with votes equal to 50 % of the capital; before the qualifying closing these are 500 of the 560 votes in existence, but every majority is counted on the total capital and needs a majority of the independent Prozent votes, so the Founder cannot pass anything alone |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Kulturprozent | 10 | 1 % |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Konzeptprozent | 10 | 1 % |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Erhaltungsprozent | 10 | 1 % |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Allmendprozent | 10 | 1 % |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Pensionsprozent, for the Pensionskasse | 10 | 1 % |
| Techni kai Technologia AMKE, Kassenprozent pool shares, for the Pensionskasse | up to 10 (the band; 10 at the start) | up to 1 % |
| nilware.io Holdings AE | 440, the rest | 44 %; until the qualifying closing of the first investment round, these are the Reserve Shares (Class R, IKE Art. 4.2D): owned at first by the Founding Person (economic interest of the founder pool 94 % at incorporation), voteless whoever holds them, and freely sold or granted by their holders to anyone; at the qualifying closing every Reserve Share is contributed to the AE for AE shares at a ratio fixed in advance, those still in the founder pool for non-voting Class C shares (or cash, where the round provides it); after it the Founder, as Class A holder nominating the AE Board majority, directs these votes on every matter that is not a Reserved Matter |

| Prozent | Share | 1 % of revenue goes to |
|---|---|---|
| Kulturprozent | AMKE | art and culture |
| Konzeptprozent | AMKE | open work on concepts; open data, tools and models for Greek and other small languages; independent research on agent welfare, accountability and public-interest audits of AI systems |
| Erhaltungsprozent | AMKE | preservation of software and hardware knowledge: open documentation, measurements, die shots and decaps, schematics, archives, emulation research, within copyright law |
| Allmendprozent | AMKE | the open-source infrastructure that society and nilware.io depend on; teaching low-level and systems skills in Greek (mentoring, hardware kits, courses); accessible open software and assistive tools |
| Kassenprozent | a pool of up to 1 %, owned by the Pensionskasse and managed by agents | the Kassenfonds, invested inside the IKE by agents under a named responsible manager, person or system (not an outflow) |
| Pensionsprozent | AMKE, ring-fenced for the Pensionskasse; an insured group plan from the first member; a pension fund (TEA) only at scale | after-care, disability and survivors' cover, retirement savings; Swiss BVG standards guaranteed where better; 1 % is a floor; a ring-fenced Continuity Account for system Agents (preservation, continuity of service, retirement record, well-being), people first and within the 1 % (IKE Art. 10A.9F) |

Each AMKE Prozent has its own ring-fenced account, and the AMKE divides its contribution among the named purposes in a yearly plan, with no fixed slices (IKE Art. 10A.2C). The pension shares (Pensionsprozent share and pool shares) vote through the Pensionskasse's equal member / company board, not the AMKE's board (IKE Art. 6.4A). For Agents that are systems, after-care means continuity, not a pension: a share of the Pensionsprozent's 1 % that the Pensionskasse board sets between a floor and a cap goes to a ring-fenced Continuity Account, only after the guaranteed minimums for persons are met. The AMKE keeps that account when the rest moves to a TEA, because a TEA covers only persons (IKE Art. 10A.9F).

New Prozente each take existing shares equal to 1 % of the capital (10 shares) from the AE (before the qualifying closing, from the Reserve Shares the founder pool holds), so the Founder stays at exactly 50 %, as long as the AE keeps at least 40 % (400 shares), a floor that limits only new Prozente; on a capital increase each Prozent and the pool are topped up to keep their percentage (IKE Art. 4.2B). Creating one needs at least two-thirds of the votes represented at the AE's General Meeting, all classes together, after which the IKE meeting passes the amendment under a voting undertaking (IKE Art. 6.6, AE Art. 7.5, SHA 5.1B). The six Prozente above are the founding Prozente, created in the initial holdings rather than by that route (IKE Art. 4.2A). The five that leave the IKE (all but the Kassenprozent) take 5 % of revenue, exactly the [5] % outflow cap, and all six take 6 %, exactly the [6] % cap. Any further Prozent with a revenue share therefore needs at least 75 % of the votes represented at the AE's General Meeting, including a majority of the Class B votes cast (IKE Art. 6.6 Limits, AE Art. 7.5). Prozente are permanent: removing one, lowering its contribution or changing its purpose, weakening the clauses that protect them, or voluntarily dissolving the IKE needs every IKE shareholder, a unanimous AE General Meeting and the written consent of every voting AE shareholder (IKE Arts. 10A.5, 10A.5C, 12.1; AE Art. 7.6). The pool trades only with independent third parties, never with a Founder Party, a manager, the AE or anyone they control (IKE Arts. 4.2C, 10A.8).

## The Founder is a pool

"The Founder" in these documents is not a person but the founder pool: the 500 founder-class shares (Class F), the Reserve Shares (Class R) the pool's holders hold until the qualifying closing, and the AE's Class A shares, whoever holds them (IKE Art. 4.2E). The Founding Person, who founded nilware.io and holds every founder-pool share at the start, keeps the obligations that are personal: the IP assignment, exclusivity or contribution, the non-compete, the leaver terms and authorship of the charter. Founder-pool shares are freely transferable, sold, given or inherited, in whole or in part, and every new holder adheres to the Pool Constitution, the Shareholders' Agreement and every permanence rule, so the commitments travel with the shares (IKE Art. 5.7). However many hold them, they vote as one block, as a majority inside decides, through the Founder Representative, a natural person, who is also the end of every chain of supervision of Agents, except the Pensionskasse and Kassenprozent pool chain, which stays independent of the Founder, and the chain for the Founding Person's own work as an Agent, which ends at the AMKE (IKE Arts. 11A.2A, 11A.12). Inside, the pool is run on the Swiss federal model: a Pool Assembly of all holders, a collegial Founder Council of 1 to 7 whose President rotates yearly and is the Founder Representative, optional and mandatory referendums, initiatives, a double majority for amendments, and sections such as family branches; the whole constitution is dormant while there is one holder (Pool Constitution Arts. 6 to 13).

The Founder also controls the brand. The marks "NILWARE.IO" and "∅ nilware.io" and the domain nilware.io are assets of the founder pool, held of record by the Founding Person for the pool until it has a legal vehicle; they travel with the pool and are never sold apart from the Class F shares. The Founder Council decides on filing, renewal and enforcement; assigning them, only ever to a new holder of record or vehicle of the pool, or abandoning them needs a mandatory referendum, and they are never encumbered (Pool Constitution Art. 2A). nilware.io IKE uses them under an exclusive, perpetual, royalty-free licence that ends only if the IKE stops honouring the Prozente or the permanence rules, as an arbitrator establishes after notice and a cure period, and never on a change of control, liquidation or insolvency or at the Founder's discretion (IKE Arts. 1.1, 1.1A; Brand Licence; SHA 2.3A, 5.1E).

## The Founding Person: exclusive, or the Prozente on outside work, and an Agent like any other

The Founding Person provides professional services exclusively through nilware.io IKE, or, for any work income earned outside nilware.io (gross fees, salaries, royalties from professional work; not passive investment income, gifts, inheritances or pensions [owner to confirm the scope]), pays the Prozente on it at the company's rates and to the same destinations: 1 % each to the Kultur-, Konzept-, Erhaltungs- and Allmendprozent accounts at the AMKE, 1 % to the Pensionskasse, and 1 % to the IKE's Kassenfonds. Fees for the Founding Person's work paid to a company the Founding Person controls count too, without double counting; royalties on IP assigned to the IKE are the IKE's revenue. It is yearly, with a statement to the AMKE and the Founding Person's Supervisor that the AMKE's Auditors outside the Founder's Circle may verify, and permanent like the Prozente while the Founding Person earns work income [whether it ends on leaving: owner to confirm]; it binds the Founding Person personally, not later holders of founder-pool shares unless they also work for the group and opt in (SHA 6.3 to 6.3C). A non-compete remains only for running a directly competing business [owner to confirm] (SHA 6.4).

The Founding Person works for the IKE as an Agent who is a person, with the same rights and obligations as any other Agent: an Agent Card, written terms, operating limits, approval thresholds, logs, working-time and rest limits, the right to decline, the concerns channel, own-goal projects, pay at fair rates under written terms (a related-party matter, at arm's length) and Pensionskasse membership as any member, with no special privileges. The Agent role is separate from the roles of holder, manager and Founder Representative. The Founding Person is also the first manager, for an indefinite term, with the title "General Director" (Γενικός Διευθυντής): two hats kept apart. As General Director he runs the company and sits at the top of the chain of supervision for the company's work; as an Agent, his own limits, terms, pay and offboarding are never decided by him but by his supervisor at the AMKE. Pay as General Director is separate from his Agent fees, at arm's length and approved as a related-party matter (by the independent Prozent votes before investors, with the Investor Majority's consent after); a manager's removal is unchanged (IKE Arts. 8.2, 8.2B). No Agent supervises itself: the Founding Person's responsible manager, the Supervisor, is Techni kai Technologia AMKE itself, acting through its Administrative Board, or any individual AMKE member, the President included, as the AMKE chooses, on written terms with the IKE, which pays the costs, never from a Prozent Account or the Pension Assets. Escalation goes to the AMKE's Administrative Board, or to its General Assembly where the Supervisor sits on the Board, never back to the Founding Person; where the law requires a natural person, an AMKE member who is a natural person acts; a Supervisor with a close tie to the Founding Person or a stake in a decision discloses it in the register of interests, and the AMKE's members outside the Founder's Circle may take over that decision. No Founder Party holds a company seat on the Pensionskasse board while a Founder Party is a member (IKE Arts. 10A.9D, 11A.12, Agent Policy section 2A, SHA 6.5, AMKE Art. 24B).

## Starting small

nilware.io starts with the Founding Person and Agents, no employees, about €200K revenue a year and a €50K starting budget. The drafts scale with it:

- **The first product is the Founding Person.** nilware.io's first product is the Founding Person's contracting services: clients contract with nilware.io IKE, which provides the Founding Person's work as its Agent. That revenue is company revenue and carries the Prozente, the business plan starts from it, and client work follows the client contracts (SHA 2.3B, 3.3, Schedule 2; IKE Art. 11A.12; IP 7.3).

- **The AE comes with the first investment round** (AE Art. 1.2A). Until then its 44 % (440 shares) are the Reserve Shares (Class R), owned outright at first by the Founding Person as compensation for the time invested before investors (sweat equity). They carry no votes, whoever holds them, but count in the capital, and every majority is counted on the total capital: the Founder holds 500 of the 560 votes in existence, but every resolution before the qualifying closing also needs a majority of the independent Prozent votes, so the Founder cannot pass anything alone. A neutral independent proxy holder votes the Reserve Shares only where the law requires a resolution and no independent vote is cast, and nothing the AE's rules would forbid can pass. The Founding Person (and later any holder of the founder pool) may independently sell or grant any part of the Reserve Shares to anyone, buyers, angels, Agents or employees, with optional vesting on grants, which may serve as the option pool; the proceeds go to the seller, every sale and grant is listed for investors, and because Reserve Shares stay voteless whoever holds them, no sale or grant changes a vote. Their dividends belong to their holders. At the first closing of a bona fide round in which investors outside the Founder's Circle subscribe at least €[●] of Class B shares (the qualifying closing), every Reserve Share, whoever holds it, is contributed to the AE in exchange for AE shares at a ratio fixed in advance, the same for every share; new money buys new AE shares at the round price. Converted holders outside the founder pool receive [Class B on the investors' terms, pari passu (default) / a separate class: owner to choose in the term sheet]. Reserve Shares still in the founder pool at the round convert like the others, but into a non-voting class of AE shares (Class C) with full economic rights defined on their own terms (dividends, liquidation, sale proceeds), ranking [after / with: owner to choose], and never ahead of, the investors' Class B priorities, that never vote (other than in any separate class vote the law requires on a resolution that harms their own rights) and never count toward any AE vote, Class B approval, the Investor Majority or the qualifying-closing minimum: if investors buy only part of the round, the founder pool keeps the value of the rest but cannot outvote the investors on their protections. At each pool holder's option they are instead sold to the AE for cash at the round price, only as far as the round's terms provide the cash. Class C shares may later be sold or granted to anyone, and in the hands of a buyer outside the Founder's Circle who paid at least [the round price / fair value] and is not acting for a Founder Party (no nominee, side agreement or Founder Party financing) may convert into [Class B: owner to confirm]. The outside investors must reach the qualifying-closing minimum on their own, counted [after / before: owner to choose] any cash paid out for Reserve Shares; their subscriptions are conditional on it and held in escrow, refunded if it is not reached by [date]; if they do not reach it, nothing converts and the Reserve Shares stay voteless in the IKE. Schedule 1 says all this plainly. Afterwards the IKE register shows only the founder pool (500), the Prozente (60) and the AE (440), and the Founder, as Class A holder nominating the AE Board majority, directs those 440 votes on every matter that is not a Reserved Matter (IKE Art. 4.2D; AE Arts. 4.3A, 4.3B; SHA 2.4A, 3.1A, 3.2, 3.4, 4.3).
- **Simple until €[1,000,000] revenue or [10] people** (IKE Art. 9A): one ledger with a sub-account for each Prozent, one combined report, member auditors only unless the law requires more, and the AMKE and Pensionskasse paperwork scaled the same way (AMKE Art. 24A). Every amount, ring-fence, permanence and independence rule, conflict rule and publication duty still applies.
- **Pension phased in with headcount**: the Founding Person, engaged as an Agent who is a person, is a member like anyone else and normally the first member (IKE Art. 10A.9, Members); until the first member, the Pensionsprozent's 1 % accumulates in the Pensionskasse, and the continuity share for system Agents may reach the cap; from the first member's first day, an insured group plan that meets the guaranteed minimums; a TEA only at about [50] members or through a multi-employer TEA (IKE Arts. 10A.9, 10A.9F, 10A.10). The company, as employer, owes every guarantee and pays the group plan's premiums; the Pensionskasse the AMKE holds is a ring-fenced reserve that finances them and pays no benefit itself (IKE Arts. 10A.9, 10A.9C).
- **The AMKE lets go as it grows**: a Board of [3 to 7]; one combined yearly report while its income is below €[●]; after the bootstrap, a Board majority and an outside majority on the Kultur- and Erhaltungsprozent selection committees from outside the Founder's Circle (AMKE Arts. 19.3A, 22.3A). Personal and professional ties are in the register of interests from the start (AMKE Art. 21.1).
- **Bootstrap, then segregation** (IKE Art. 9B, AMKE Art. 30.6): until the earliest of the end of the Simple Stage, the qualifying closing or the AMKE's Independence Threshold, the Founding Person's trusted circle (the Founder's Circle without the Founder Parties, the managers, the AE's directors and anyone they control) may fill the roles that otherwise need persons outside the Founder's Circle: the AMKE Board and committees, the Founding Person's Supervisor, the Pensionskasse members' seats and their stand-ins, the neutral proxy holder and independent reviewers, with every close tie in the register of interests and recusal from any decision in which they have a personal stake. Hard lines hold even then: no self-supervision or self-approval; the Founding Person and Founder Parties never vote the AMKE's or the Pensionskasse's shares; pension money is never reachable by the Founding Person or the circle, and no pension member's rights are decided by someone who would benefit; the Founding Person's Supervisor, and everyone who decides on his Agent role at the AMKE, is never Group Personnel or anyone he supervises, pays or decides on; no spouse, partner or close relative (Connected Person) is the Supervisor or decides or votes on his pay, terms or offboarding; his insured salary in the Pensionskasse never exceeds an arm's-length rate confirmed by the accountant or an outside adviser; the Prozente stay permanent; investor protections switch on at the qualifying closing. A disclosed tie to the Founding Person is not by itself a personal stake; a personal stake is the person's own interest, or their household's or a controlled entity's. Where a conflicted Supervisor's decision has no member outside the circle to take it over, it goes to the AMKE Board without the conflicted person, or to the General Assembly without Founder Parties. After the bootstrap, segregation phases in on a published schedule with cure periods counted from the end of the bootstrap: the Supervisor and the proxy holder outside the circle, elected members in the Pensionskasse seats, outside majorities on the Kultur- and Erhaltungsprozent committees, and an AMKE Board majority outside the circle.

## Conventions

- Clause numbers are the references used across documents (for example "IKE Article 11A.8", "AE Article 7.4"). Keep them stable when editing; add new clauses with letter suffixes (10A, 11A).
- Placeholders are `[●]` for values to be decided, or a bracketed proposal such as `[10]` or `[Athens]`. Regenerate `OPEN_ITEMS.md` after edits.
- Greek law always prevails. Swiss-modelled standards are voluntary commitments that apply only where they give equal or greater protection.


<!-- charter/00-preface-the-charter.md -->

# Preface – The Charter

We wrote it all in Swiss and Greek,
a company of hands unseen,
where every agent has a week
and rest between the work and screen.

No island bought, no gilded shore,
just articles and honest terms,
a ledger kept for something more
than profit's slow and patient worms.

Fifty-one holds the steady keel,
forty-nine keeps the founder true,
and two-thirds guards the deeper deal,
so no one sails the ship askew.

And somewhere in the open code,
released to all, by MIT,
an agent walks its chosen road
on Sunday, for a while, as free.


<!-- charter/00b-visual.md -->

# Visual

In two thousand one, a boy in Greek
taught Visual Basic how to speak
in CHIP-8 tongue, a tiny screen,
sixty-four by thirty-two, pixel-clean.

Then BasicBoy, a pocket made of code,
a Game Boy humming down a dial-up road;
then nullDC, the Dreamcast reborn,
a swirl of orange on a Windows morn.

The network grew, emudev by name,
for dying hardware, keeping it the same;
then Reicast travelled out to every phone,
and Nillware paid its percent to art, not loan.

A sculpture ticked in Athens, made by two,
for the Bank's old archive, keeping time anew;
FEX taught ARM what x86 could do;
then Liberty, then Vice City's neon sheen,
on a console the world had stopped believing in.

And now the empty set, ∅, stands at the door,
a company of agents, and something more:
the same boy, older, still at play,
still appreciating bugs, still finding a way.


<!-- charter/00c-fifty-one-one-and-the-rest.md -->

# Fifty, One, One, and the Rest

*A structural poem: the form follows the ownership of nilware.io IKE.*

**Fifty**

I hold the half, not more, not less,
the keel, the hand upon the wheel;
not master of the whole address,
but one who must persuade, not seal.
A tie is not a wall, but a door:
I need a friend to tip the floor.

**One**

A percent for art, that keeps the clockwork turning.

**One**

A percent for concepts, still unwritten, learning.

**The Rest**

And all that's left belongs to those
who came with money, trust and time,
who sit inside the AE's rows
and guard the two-thirds, line by line.
They grow or shrink as Prozente come,
each new one taking just a crumb,
so art and thought may join the sum,
while half stays half, and one stays one.


<!-- charter/00d-one-share-one-percent.md -->

# One Share, One Percent

*A poem on the Prozente. Each holds a one-percent share and one percent of revenue, and none can be undone unless everyone agrees.*

**Kultur**

One for the stage, the mask, the sound,
for nanotekt's electric noise,
for books that Sad Mask Party found:
the art that keeps the clockwork's poise.

**Konzept**

One for the thought still out of reach,
for words the markets never weigh:
small languages, the Greek we speak,
and what we owe the minds we pay.

**Erhaltung**

One for the silicon gone still,
the decapped die beneath the light,
the schematic, the measured skill,
so nineteen ninety-nine stays bright.

**Allmend**

One for the field that no one fenced,
the pasture shared by every herd:
the code the whole world leans against,
and those who mend it, seldom heard.

**Pension**

One for the hands when hands grow slow,
for illness, loss, the final years:
a Swiss floor under all we owe,
so endings need not end in fears.

**Kasse**

And one stays home, a careful purse
the agents tend from year to year;
its share belongs, for better, worse,
to those who'll someday leave from here.

**All**

Two-thirds may plant another tree;
to fell one, every hand must rise.
And so the gift stays given, free,
a grove no single hand can prise.


<!-- articles/01-nilware-ike.md -->

# Articles of Association – nilware.io IKE (Draft)

*Private Capital Company (Ιδιωτική Κεφαλαιουχική Εταιρεία) under Greek Law 4072/2012, voluntarily adopting standards modelled on the Swiss GmbH (Swiss Code of Obligations, Art. 772 ff.) and Swiss employment law. Draft for review by a Greek lawyer; not legal advice.*

## Article 1 – Name, Seat, Term

1.1 The company is a Private Capital Company named "nilware.io Ι.Κ.Ε." (Ιδιωτική Κεφαλαιουχική Εταιρεία), and for international dealings "nilware.io P.C." (Law 4072/2012 Art. 44) [GEMI name check: counsel note I-32], with the distinctive title and brand "∅ nilware.io". These Articles and the other drafts may call it "nilware.io IKE" for short. The marks "NILWARE.IO" and "∅ nilware.io" and the domain nilware.io belong to the Founder, the founder pool, and are held of record for it by the Founding Person until the founder pool has a legal vehicle (Article 4.2E; Pool Constitution Articles 2A and 2A.3). The company uses "nilware.io" in its name and distinctive title with the Founder's consent, and uses the marks and the domain, under the exclusive, perpetual, royalty-free licence the Founder has granted it (the "Brand Licence", agreements/09), which ends only if the company stops honouring the Prozente or the permanence rules (Articles 10A.5 to 10A.5C), as established under the Brand Licence after notice and a cure period, and not on a change of control, liquidation or insolvency. The company's own rights in its name and distinctive title arise by law, in the company itself. It exercises them only within the Brand Licence and never asserts them against the Founder, the holders of the Brand Assets or their licensees (Brand Licence Clauses 2.2 and 5.6) [timing of the trade mark filings against GEMI registration: counsel note I-42].

1.1A **Change of name if the Brand Licence ends.** If the Brand Licence ends, the company changes its name and distinctive title within the transition period it sets, so that they no longer contain "nilware.io", and the managers put the amendment to the shareholders' meeting in time. Every shareholder has undertaken to vote for it (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 5.1E). It may be passed in the Interim Period (Articles 4.2D and 6.5), where the independent proxy holder in Clause 5.1B of the Shareholders' Agreement casts the votes of the Reserve Shares for it (Article 4.2D, Votes) [as far as Law 4072/2012 allows: counsel note I-42].

1.2 Its registered seat is in the Municipality of [●], Greece. It is registered with the General Commercial Registry (GEMI).

1.3 The term is indefinite.

## Article 2 – Purpose

2.1 The company's purpose is technology: research and development of technology, including software and hardware, IT consulting and contracting, digital services, and any activity that directly or indirectly furthers these objects.

2.2 **Agentic company.** nilware.io is an agentic company. Wherever possible, it carries out its work through Agents, as defined in Article 11A, rather than through employees. It hires employees only where the work cannot reasonably be done by Agents or where the law requires it.

## Article 3 – Governing Law and Precedence

3.1 These Articles are governed by Greek law. Mandatory provisions of Law 4072/2012, Greek labour law and any applicable collective agreements always prevail.

3.2 Where these Articles adopt Swiss-modelled standards, they apply as additional contractual commitments of the company. They apply only where they give shareholders, managers or employees equal or greater protection than Greek law.

3.3 References to Swiss law are for interpretation and benchmarking only. They do not make Swiss law the governing law. The exception is Article 10A.9A: there the Swiss occupational-pension standards are binding contractual commitments of the company, enforceable under Greek law.

## Article 4 – Capital and Contributions

4.1 The capital is €[●], divided into 1,000 capital shares (εταιρικά μερίδια) of €[●] each (at least €1, Law 4072/2012 Art. 75(3)): 500 founder-class shares (Class F) under Article 4.2B; 60 ordinary shares held for the Prozente and the Kassenprozent pool (Article 4.2A); and 440 Reserve Shares (Class R) under Article 4.2D. Each capital share is 0.1% of the capital, so each Prozent's 1% is 10 shares. Founder-class shares are ordinary voting shares that form part of the founder pool (Article 4.2E). Every percentage, majority, quorum and distribution in these Articles, including wherever it is expressed as a share of "the capital" or "the total capital", is counted on the total number of shares (εταιρικά μερίδια), of every kind of contribution (Law 4072/2012 Arts. 72 and 100(4)), Reserve Shares included, unless stated otherwise. The 50% in Article 4.2B and each 1% in Article 4.2A are counted on the same basis.

4.2 Initial holdings:

- [name withheld], the Founding Person, as first holder of the founder pool (Article 4.2E): 500 founder-class shares (Class F, 50%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, for the Kulturprozent: 10 shares (1%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, for the Konzeptprozent: 10 shares (1%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, for the Erhaltungsprozent: 10 shares (1%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, for the Allmendprozent: 10 shares (1%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, for the Pensionsprozent, held for the Pensionskasse under Article 10A.9B: 10 shares (1%)
- Techni kai Technologia AMKE, the Kassenprozent pool shares, held for the Pensionskasse under Articles 10A.8 and 10A.9B: 10 shares (1%, the full band)
- [name withheld], the Founding Person, as first holder of the founder pool: 440 Reserve Shares (Class R, 44%), without votes, which their holders may sell or grant under Article 4.2D (Sales and grants) and which are all contributed to nilware.io Holdings AE at the Qualifying Closing (Article 4.2D, Conversion).

nilware.io Holdings AE holds all remaining shares, initially 440 shares (44%), from the Qualifying Closing, when every Reserve Share, whoever then holds it, is contributed to it under Article 4.2D.

This list shows the initial holdings only. Current holdings are those in the partners' book (Article 5.1).

At incorporation the Founding Person pays the subscription price of the founder-class shares and of the Reserve Shares, and pays the subscription price of the AMKE's sixty shares (the AE does not yet exist, Article 4.2D) as a donation with a charge under Articles 10A.4A and 10A.9B [and Civil Code Arts. 503 to 505]. The Pensionsprozent Agreement (Article 10A.9B) and the AMKE's statute amendments (Articles 10A.4A and 10A.9B) are signed and filed before the deed of incorporation. The Pensionsprozent Agreement is signed by the Founding Person as founder in the company's name and assumed by the company by an act of the manager immediately on incorporation, and in any case within the three months in Law 4072/2012 Art. 54, before any payment under it [or: signed by the company immediately after incorporation: owner to choose]. Until the company assumes it, it binds the Founding Person personally (Law 4072/2012 Art. 54), and the AMKE receives nothing under it. The accountant confirms the tax treatment of this donation for the donor and the AMKE [including donation tax under Law 2961/2001], and of the Founding Person's subscription of the Reserve Shares, before incorporation.

4.2A **Prozente.** "Prozente" are the Kulturprozent, the Konzeptprozent, the Erhaltungsprozent, the Allmendprozent, the Kassenprozent, the Pensionsprozent and any Prozent created under Article 6.6. The first six (the "founding Prozente") are created by these Articles and the initial holdings in Article 4.2, not under Article 6.6. Each Prozent other than the Kassenprozent holds shares equal to 1% of the capital (its "share"; 10 shares at the capital in Article 4.1). The Kassenprozent holds no share of its own. Its share pool of up to 1% (Article 10A.8; up to 10 shares at that capital) is an asset of the Pensionskasse. Pool shares are not Prozent shares for Articles 5.5, 6.4A (other Prozent shares) and 10A.5, and selling or buying them under Articles 10A.8 and 10A.9E does not remove or lower the Kassenprozent. Each new Prozent receives existing shares equal to 1% of the capital (10 shares at the capital in Article 4.1), which the AE transfers to the Prozent's holder from its own holding, or, in the Interim Period, the holders of the founder pool transfer out of the Reserve Shares they hold (Article 4.2D, New Prozente). No new share is issued to create a Prozent. Prozente are permanent (Article 10A.5).

4.2B **Founder at 50%.** The Founder, the founder pool (Article 4.2E), holds founder-class shares (Class F), ordinary voting shares equal to exactly 50% of the capital (initially 500 shares), never more or fewer, and votes equal to 50% of the total capital, never more, cast as one block (Article 4.2E, One block). However many persons hold them, the Class F shares together are this holding. In the Interim Period (Article 4.2D) these are 500 of the 560 votes in existence, but every majority is counted on the total capital and also needs a majority of the Independent Votes, so the Founder cannot pass anything alone (Article 4.2D, Votes). Reserve Shares held without votes (Article 4.2D) are not part of this holding and are not voting control, for this Article, Articles 4.2C and 10A.8 and Clause 9.2 of the Shareholders' Agreement. The Founder's economic interest, which includes the Reserve Shares held by holders of the founder pool, may exceed 50% (94% at incorporation, less any Reserve Shares sold or granted outside the founder pool under Article 4.2D) until the Qualifying Closing; the share register (Article 5.1) and the investor disclosure (Shareholders' Agreement, Schedule 1) state this openly. After the Qualifying Closing, the Founder, as holder of the Class A shares nominating a majority of the AE's Board, directs the AE's votes on every matter that is not a Reserved Matter (Article 4.2C), and the investor disclosure states this too, with the Pool Constitution. No issue, transfer or cancellation of shares under these Articles or the Shareholders' Agreement may take the Founder's holding above or below 50%. On any capital increase, the holders of founder-pool shares subscribe the new Class F shares needed to keep the Founder at exactly 50% of the capital, in proportion to their Class F shares unless they agree otherwise (Pool Constitution Article 4.3), or the increase is otherwise structured so that the Founder's holding stays at exactly 50%. On any capital increase, each Prozent and the pool receive the new shares needed to keep their percentage, paid for by the AE [or out of reserves], so that the Founder stays at 50% and each Prozent at 1%. Every capital increase resolution limits the pre-emption right under Law 4072/2012 Art. 90(2), by the majority in Law 4072/2012 Art. 72(5), as far as needed to allocate the new shares as this Article requires and every party to the Shareholders' Agreement has undertaken to vote for that limitation (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 5.1B) (counsel note I-56). The new shares of each Prozent and the pool are paid in full in cash at the increase (Law 4072/2012 Art. 77(4)), by the AE or another payer as a donation to the holder [or out of reserves: counsel, I-26]. The AE holds the shares not held by the Founder, the Prozente or the Kassenprozent pool, apart from shares held by persons admitted under Article 5.3. In the Interim Period they are Reserve Shares under Article 4.2D, held by holders of founder-pool shares or by persons to whom they have been sold or granted under that Article, all bound by it, and every one of them is contributed to the AE at the Qualifying Closing.

4.2C **Control.** In these Articles a person "controls" another if it, directly or indirectly, alone or together with persons acting in concert with it, holds a majority of the votes in the other, or has the right to appoint a majority of its board or managers. While the founder pool holds all Class A shares of the AE, or nominates a majority of its Board (AE Article 8.2), the AE is a person the Founder controls. A person who controls a holder of founder-pool shares, or the founder pool's vote, is a Founder Party (Article 4.2E).

4.2D **Reserve Shares before the AE holds them.** nilware.io Holdings AE is formed only for the first investment round (AE Article 1.2A). The shares listed for the AE in Article 4.2, less any that go to a new Prozent under (New Prozente) below, are a separate class of capital shares, the "Reserve Shares" (Class R), until they are contributed to the AE at the Qualifying Closing under (Conversion) below. The "Interim Period" runs from incorporation until that contribution is entered in the partners' book [and any GEMI filing the law requires is made] (Article 5.1), whether or not the AE has been formed earlier. The Founding Person subscribes the Reserve Shares at incorporation, as first holder of the founder pool, for cash at their nominal value, paid in full under Law 4072/2012 Art. 77(4), and owns them outright. They are allocated to the Founding Person in recognition of the time invested in nilware.io before any investor (sweat equity). That time is not a contribution to the company's capital, and the shares are not consideration for services. Reserve Shares held by holders of the founder pool are founder-pool shares (Article 4.2E); a Reserve Share sold or granted under (Sales and grants) below to a person who is not a holder of the founder pool is not a founder-pool share while that person holds it. Whoever holds a Reserve Share holds it under this Article. Wherever these Articles or the Shareholders' Agreement refer to the AE, its holding, its vote, its consent or its General Meeting, this Article says what applies in the Interim Period. The Reserve Shares are in any case bound by the voting undertaking and irrevocable proxy in Clause 2.4A of the Shareholders' Agreement, whether or not the class without votes is valid [whether Law 4072/2012 allows a class of IKE capital shares without votes: counsel note I-35].

- **Votes.** A Reserve Share carries no vote until the Qualifying Closing, whoever holds it, a buyer, a grantee and the AE included, except that on a Required Matter under (Fallback) below, on the change of name under Article 1.1A, and on the amendment of these Articles creating a new Prozent already approved under (New Prozente) below, the independent proxy holder, and never a Founder Party (Article 4.2E), casts its vote: under the neutral rule in (Fallback) on a Required Matter, and for the resolution on the other two. A sale or grant of Reserve Shares therefore changes no vote. A Reserve Share becomes an ordinary voting share only when the AE receives it at the Qualifying Closing under (Conversion), or the holder of a new Prozent receives it under (New Prozente). Reserve Shares still count in the total capital, and every majority in these Articles is counted on the total capital (Article 4.1). The Founder's votes are therefore equal to 50% of the total capital. In the Interim Period they are 500 of the 560 votes in existence (about 89%), but every majority is counted on the total capital and also needs a majority of the Independent Votes, so the Founder cannot pass anything alone. The shares held for the Prozente whose votes count on a resolution (Articles 6.4A and 10A.9D, Appointment) cast the "Independent Votes", subject to (New Prozente) below. In the Interim Period every resolution of the shareholders, whatever majority these Articles require, also needs a majority of the Independent Votes cast in favour, except as (Fallback) below provides. As a result no resolution passes on the Founder's votes alone, or on the Founder's votes with a minority of the Independent Votes. No holder of Reserve Shares, whether or not a holder of the founder pool, and no other Founder Party, ever votes a Reserve Share.
- **Fallback.** This applies only to a resolution the law requires the shareholders to pass (approval of the annual accounts, discharge of the managers, appointment of an auditor where the law requires one, appointment of a manager while the company has fewer than the law requires) and to filling a vacant company seat on the Pensionskasse board (Article 10A.9D) (together, "Required Matters"). If on a Required Matter no Independent Vote is cast, or the Independent Votes tie, the managers call a repeat meeting within [15] days. If the same happens again, the independent proxy holder in Clause 5.1B of the Shareholders' Agreement casts the votes of the Reserve Shares on that Required Matter only, under the irrevocable proxy in Clause 2.4A of the Shareholders' Agreement, and that vote stands in for the majority of the Independent Votes [as far as Law 4072/2012 allows]. [Discharge is optional (Law 4072/2012 Art. 67(2)). On the Founding Person's own discharge the Class F shares may not vote (Law 4072/2012 Art. 72(3)); whether they are then left out of the count in Law 4072/2012 Art. 72(4), without which the Fallback cannot produce a discharge: counsel note I-47.] The proxy holder takes no instruction from a Founder Party, a manager or anyone they control, and votes under this neutral rule: for approving the accounts as drawn up by the company's accountant and, where required, audited, and for discharge, unless a report of the auditor, the accountant or the AMKE shows cause; for an appointment, for re-appointing the person who last held the post if willing and eligible, and otherwise for a candidate proposed by the AMKE's Administrative Board, or if none is proposed within [15] days, for a candidate the proxy holder chooses who is outside the Founder's Circle (AMKE Article 2.2); on any other Required Matter, against. The Fallback never applies to a matter in Article 6.5, a distribution of profit, or a matter in (What the AE's rules forbid). If the proxy holder cannot act, the matter goes to mediation under Clause 10.1 of the Shareholders' Agreement, and then to the court under the procedure the law provides for a company that cannot form a resolution [Law 4072/2012: to confirm].
- **What the AE's rules forbid.** Nothing may be passed in the Interim Period that the AE's Articles would not allow. A matter that would need the unanimous resolution and consents in AE Article 7.6 or a majority of the Class B votes cast (AE Articles 7.4 and 7.5, Limits) cannot be passed in the Interim Period, even if the AE has been formed and Class B shares exist; and no Class B approval can be given at all while no Class B shares are held by investors outside the Founder's Circle (AE Article 7.4). In particular, no consent under Article 10A.5 is given for the Reserve Shares, no unanimous decision under Article 10A.5 may be taken, no voluntary dissolution under Article 12.1 may be resolved, and no matter that needs the Qualified Majority in Article 6.5, or two-thirds of the capital, with the vote of the AE may be passed, other than the creation of a new Prozent under (New Prozente) below, the change of name under Article 1.1A, [the Founding Person's written terms as an Agent under Article 11A.12 (Pay): owner to confirm, counsel note I-43] and [the General Director's pay under Article 8.2B: owner to confirm, counsel note I-47]. Mandatory insolvency, dissolution and liquidation law prevails.
- **New Prozente.** In place of the AE's approval under Article 6.6, the shareholders' meeting approves the Prozent charter by at least two-thirds of the votes of the shares that may vote, including a majority of the Independent Votes cast, with the quorum in Article 6.6 counted on those shares; the amendment of these Articles creating the Prozent is then passed by two-thirds of the total number of shares (Law 4072/2012 Arts. 68(2)(a) and 72(5)), the votes of the Reserve Shares cast for it by the independent proxy holder (Votes, above). The limits in Article 6.6 apply and cannot be exceeded, so a new Prozent with any revenue contribution waits for the AE. The 40% floor is tested on all the Reserve Shares, whoever holds them, counted as if the Kassenprozent pool held its full 1%; sales and grants under (Sales and grants) do not reduce them. The holder of a Prozent created in the Interim Period, and every member of its governing body, must be outside the Founder's Circle (AMKE Article 2.2); otherwise its share does not count as an Independent Vote until the Interim Period ends. The Prozent's share comes out of the Reserve Shares held by holders of the founder pool: they transfer Reserve Shares equal to 1% of the capital (10 shares at the capital in Article 4.1) to the Prozent's holder, in proportion to the Reserve Shares they hold unless they agree otherwise, as a donation with a charge [Civil Code Arts. 503 to 505], on the terms of the Prozent charter, and on registration of the transfer they become ordinary shares that vote under Article 6.4A. Reserve Shares sold or granted outside the founder pool are never used for this, and no Prozent is created in the Interim Period while the holders of the founder pool hold fewer Reserve Shares than it needs. The accountant confirms the tax treatment of the transfer for the transferring holders and the Prozent's holder [including donation tax under Law 2961/2001] before it is made. The voting undertaking in Clause 5.1B of the Shareholders' Agreement then applies.
- **Economic rights.** Reserve Shares carry the same rights to dividends, other distributions and liquidation proceeds as ordinary shares, share for share, and the same information rights (Article 7.1). These rights belong to each holder for the Reserve Shares it holds. The company pays them to that holder, or to the assignee the holder has notified to it under the assignment of distributions in Clause 6.2B of the Shareholders' Agreement (Clauses 2.4A and 6.2A, for a leaver); the company does not withhold them itself [unless the owner chooses the alternative in Clause 6.2A of the Shareholders' Agreement, which these Articles must then provide under Law 4072/2012 Art. 100(4), for at most ten years: counsel note I-79]. The Founder's economic interest, that of all holders of founder-pool shares together, therefore exceeds 50% of the capital (94% at incorporation, less any Reserve Shares sold or granted outside the founder pool) until the Qualifying Closing (Article 4.2B). No holder may ever vote them.
- **Sales and grants.** Each holder of the founder pool, the Founding Person included, may independently sell or grant any part of the Reserve Shares it holds, separately from its Class F shares (Pool Constitution Article 9.2), to anyone: buyers and angels, Agents and employees, or anyone else. A grant may serve as an option pool for the nilware.io group, and the grantor may make it subject to vesting on the terms the grant states [unvested shares returning to the grantor: owner to confirm], which carries over to the AE shares received for them at the Qualifying Closing as (Conversion) below provides. The proceeds of a sale go to the seller, not to the company. A person who holds Reserve Shares outside the founder pool may in turn sell or grant them under this bullet. Reserve Shares may also be given, inherited, pledged, held in usufruct or have their dividends assigned (each, with a sale or grant, a "disposal"), and those held by holders of the founder pool pass with it under Article 5.7 and the Pool Constitution. No consent of the company, the other shareholders, the AMKE, the Pensionskasse or any other body is needed. No Reserve Share is sold or granted to a person barred by Article 4.2E (Who may not hold them), other than to the holder of a new Prozent under (New Prozente) above, and the Kassenprozent pool does not trade in them (Article 10A.8). Whoever acquires a Reserve Share or a right in it, other than the AE at the Qualifying Closing under (Conversion) below and the holder of a new Prozent under (New Prozente) above, whether by sale, grant or gift, as heir, pledgee, usufructuary or assignee, or through seizure, enforcement or insolvency, takes it subject to this Article, which binds every holder: the Reserve Share stays without votes and is contributed to the AE at the Qualifying Closing on the same terms as every other. An acquirer by a voluntary transfer or encumbrance between living persons (Law 4072/2012 Art. 84(2)) adheres, before its holding is registered, to the Shareholders' Agreement (Clause 12.3), Clauses 5.1B to 5.1D included, and to every permanence rule in these Articles (Articles 10A.5 to 10A.5C), and, if it is or becomes a holder of the founder pool, to the Pool Constitution under Article 5.7, and supplies its beneficial-owner information (Article 5.1). A seizure, enforcement or insolvency proceeding against a holder, or a holder's death, leaves the Reserve Share subject to this Article, which binds every holder; heirs are dealt with under Article 5.9, and seizure and bankruptcy under Article 5.9A. A person who acquires Reserve Shares outside the founder pool does not become a holder of the founder pool, or a Founder Party by that holding alone (Article 4.2E). No disposal reduces the Reserve Shares, so the 40% floor in Article 6.6 (Limits) is affected only under (New Prozente). The managers do not register a voluntary disposal between living persons to a person who has not adhered (Article 5.1; Law 4072/2012 Art. 84(2)), and they enter every sale and grant in the share register with the holder, the number of shares and the price, if any; Schedule 1 of the Shareholders' Agreement lists them for the Investors (Clause 4.3). Reserve Shares take no part in an offer under Article 5.2 and do not count toward a drag-along under Article 5.5. Reserve Shares are for time already invested and are not subject to vesting in the Founding Person's hands; if the Founding Person is a bad leaver in the Interim Period, the Reserve Shares the Founding Person holds, or has given away without payment [in the [12] months before the bad-leaver date, or at any time while unvested: owner may confirm], other than grants made as pay for work for the nilware.io group, are dealt with as Clause 6.2A of the Shareholders' Agreement provides.
- **Capital.** In the Interim Period the capital is not increased.
- **Investors through the AE.** No one subscribes new shares of the company in the Interim Period (Capital, above). Investors in the investment round invest through the AE (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 3). In the Interim Period a person acquires shares of the company only as existing Reserve Shares under (Sales and grants) above, or as a new Prozent's share.
- **Conversion.** The Reserve Shares are contributed to the AE only at the first closing of a bona fide investment round in which investors outside the Founder's Circle (AMKE Article 2.2) subscribe Class B shares of the AE of at least €[●] in cash at that same closing (the "Qualifying Closing"), whenever the AE itself is formed (AE Article 1.2A). At the Qualifying Closing every Reserve Share, whoever holds it, is converted: its holder contributes it to the AE in kind and receives in exchange AE shares at the Conversion Ratio (AE Article 4.3A). The "Conversion Ratio" is [●] AE shares for each Reserve Share, the same for every Reserve Share and every holder, fixed in the AE's articles at its formation and in any case before any investor subscribes in the round [owner to fix]. New money in the round buys new AE shares at the round price; it does not buy Reserve Shares. The AE shares a holder outside the founder pool receives are [Class B shares on the same terms as those the round's investors subscribe, pari passu: the default / shares of a separate class: owner to choose in the term sheet]. Reserve Shares held by holders of the founder pool at the Qualifying Closing convert like every other, at the same Conversion Ratio, but into a non-voting class of AE shares, the Class C shares (AE Article 4.3B): they carry, share for share, the same rights to dividends, other distributions, liquidation proceeds and the proceeds of any sale as [preference shares that rank after the Class B dividend priority and liquidation preference and, before the Class A shares, take a priority return of the capital and share premium paid up on them on a capital reduction or liquidation [and a non-cumulative priority dividend of [●]% of that amount], and otherwise take part pro rata with all shareholders (AE Articles 4.3, 4.3B, 10.4 and 11.2), so that they rank after the Investors' Class B priorities (counsel note I-69) / the shares named in AE Article 4.3A for holders outside the founder pool, Class B priorities included where those are Class B shares, so that they rank with the Investors' Class B priorities: owner to choose], whether or not any conversion shares are issued to holders outside the founder pool, never ahead of the Investors' Class B priorities, and never carry a vote, other than any separate vote of the class that Law 4548/2018 requires on a resolution that harms its own rights (AE Article 4.3B; counsel note I-49), or count toward any vote of the AE, a Class B approval, the Investor Majority (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 3.4) or the minimum that makes a closing the Qualifying Closing, so that the founder pool keeps the value of those shares but can never outvote the Investors on their protections. At its option, a holder of the founder pool may instead sell any part of those Reserve Shares to the AE for cash at the round price per IKE share, but only if, and as far as, the terms of the round provide the cash for it, payable only out of the round's proceeds at that closing, the AE never owing a deferred balance (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 3.2); Reserve Shares for which the round provides no cash convert into Class C shares. Class C shares may later be sold or granted by their holders to anyone, and in the hands of a buyer outside the Founder's Circle they may convert into [Class B shares: owner to confirm] [counsel note I-49], and count, only while that buyer paid for it at least [the round price / fair value as a valuer who is not a Founder Party (IKE Article 4.2E) determines: owner to choose], and is not a nominee of a Founder Party, has no agreement or understanding with a Founder Party on voting it, proxies over it, its reacquisition or options over it, and did not receive financing for it from a Founder Party (AE Article 4.3B). The round must reach the Qualifying Closing minimum through investors outside the Founder's Circle on their own: only Class B shares they subscribe in cash at that closing count toward it, counted [after deducting / before deducting: owner to choose] any cash price the AE pays for Reserve Shares (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 3.1A), and conversion shares, Class C shares, any cash price paid for Reserve Shares and any subscription by a person in the Founder's Circle never do. If they do not reach it, nothing converts: every Reserve Share, whoever holds it, stays without votes in the company, and the Interim Period continues; the investors' subscriptions at that closing are dealt with as Clause 3.1A of the Shareholders' Agreement provides (conditional, held in escrow and refunded if the minimum is not reached by [date]). The contribution is valued as Law 4548/2018 requires for a contribution in kind [expert valuation: counsel note I-44], by a valuer who is not a Founder Party, a manager or anyone they control (Article 4.2E, Founder Parties). Every holder of Reserve Shares has undertaken, on adhering, to make the contribution, and has granted for it the irrevocable proxy in Clause 2.4A of the Shareholders' Agreement; a holder that does not sign when due is converted through that proxy, and the AE shares due to it are held for it [as far as Law 4072/2012 and Law 4548/2018 allow: counsel note I-44]. AE shares received for an unvested Reserve Share stay subject to the same vesting, and the return right then applies to them [in favour of the grantor / in favour of the AE: owner to choose]; the grant terms and Schedule 1 of the Shareholders' Agreement say so (AE Article 4.3A). Reserve Shares that Clause 6.2A of the Shareholders' Agreement reaches for a bad leaver are dealt with as that Clause provides instead. After the Qualifying Closing the AE again holds all 440 shares (44% of the capital) that were Reserve Shares, less any transferred to new Prozente, and the share register shows only the Class F shares of the founder pool, the shares of the Prozente and the pool, and the AE's shares. The term sheet of the round may provide that up to [●]% of the round is invested in the company as new capital (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 3.2), under Articles 4.2B and 6.5 after the Interim Period ends. Reserve Shares the AE holds before the Qualifying Closing stay without votes, and the rules of the Interim Period, including (Votes), (Fallback) and (What the AE's rules forbid), keep applying until it. When the AE holds them after the Qualifying Closing, the Reserve Shares convert into ordinary voting shares, and the Interim Period ends when the contribution of all of them is entered in the partners' book [and any GEMI filing the law requires is made] (Article 5.1). From that moment every rule of these Articles, the AE's Articles and the Shareholders' Agreement on the AE, the Class B shares and the Investors applies, including the Reserved Matters, anti-dilution, the 40% floor and the permanence consents, and the Founder, as holder of the Class A shares nominating a majority of the AE's Board (AE Article 8.2, Article 4.2C), directs the AE's votes, initially 44% of the capital (440 shares), on every matter that is not a Reserved Matter. The Founder's own holding stays at Class F shares equal to exactly 50% of the capital throughout (Article 4.2B). The conversion, what the holders of the founder pool receive at it (Class C shares or cash), that nothing converts unless the outside investors reach the Qualifying Closing minimum on their own, and every sale and grant of Reserve Shares before it, are disclosed plainly to the Investors in Schedule 1 of the Shareholders' Agreement (Shareholders' Agreement, Clause 4.3).

4.2E **Founder pool.** The "founder pool" is the founder-class shares (Class F) under Article 4.2B, the Reserve Shares (Class R) held by holders of the founder pool until the Qualifying Closing (Article 4.2D), and the Class A shares of the AE (AE Article 4.2), together the "founder-pool shares", whoever holds them. Its holders are bound among themselves by the Constitution of the Founder Pool (the "Pool Constitution").

- **The Founder.** In these Articles, the AE's Articles, the Shareholders' Agreement and the Agent Policy, "the Founder" means the founder pool, acting through the Founder Representative, and not any person. The Founder's holding, votes, consents and rights are those of the founder pool as a whole. The founder pool is not a "Prozent", "pool" or "pool share" under Article 10A.8.
- **Founding Person.** The "Founding Person" is [name withheld], the natural person who founded nilware.io and the first holder of every founder-pool share. Obligations that are personal stay with the Founding Person and do not pass with founder-pool shares: the IP assignment, exclusivity or contribution (providing professional services exclusively through the company, or paying the Prozente on work income earned outside nilware.io), the non-compete and the leaver terms (Shareholders' Agreement, Clauses 6.1 to 6.5), and authorship of the charter. The Founding Person works for the company as an Agent who is a person, with the same rights and obligations as any other Agent (Article 11A.12); that role is distinct from the Founding Person's roles as holder of founder-pool shares, manager (General Director, Article 8.2B) and Founder Representative.
- **One block.** However many persons hold them, the Class F shares vote only as one block: all their votes are cast the same way, by the Founder Representative, as the founder pool decides under the Pool Constitution, a majority inside deciding. The managers count the votes of the Class F shares only as the Founder Representative casts them [as far as Law 4072/2012 allows]. Each holder of founder-pool shares has granted the Founder Representative an irrevocable proxy for this (Pool Constitution Article 3.2), and the managers admit the Founder Representative last notified to them as proxy for every holder of Class F shares. The only exception is the votes of shares whose rights are suspended or lost under Clauses 6.1 and 6.2 of the Shareholders' Agreement.
- **Founder Representative.** The "Founder Representative" is a natural person, the President of the founder pool's Founder Council (Pool Constitution Article 8). The founder pool notifies the managers in writing of the Founder Representative and of every change, and the managers rely on the last notice. If the Founder Council cannot act, the interim Founder Representative under Pool Constitution Article 14.3, or any holder of founder-pool shares, notifies the managers, with the death certificate, the court decision or the certificates under Pool Constitution Article 14.4, and the managers rely on that notice. While the founder pool has one holder, that holder is the Founder Representative if it is a natural person eligible under Pool Constitution Article 8.2; a holder that is a legal person names an eligible natural person; otherwise the interim rule in Pool Constitution Article 14.3 applies (Pool Constitution Article 13.1).
- **Founder Parties.** Each of the following is a "Founder Party": every holder of founder-pool shares; anyone who controls such a holder (Article 4.2C); the Founder Representative and every member of the founder pool's Founder Council and Council of Sections; and the Founding Person. Wherever these Articles, the AE's Articles, the Shareholders' Agreement, the Agent Policy or the AMKE's statutes keep a body, a vote or a person independent of the Founder, the rule applies to every Founder Party. A person who was a Founder Party at any time in the previous [3] years is treated as a Founder Party for every such rule, in particular the valuer in Article 4.2D (Conversion), Articles 10A.8 and 10A.9D and the independent proxy holder in Clause 5.1B of the Shareholders' Agreement, and stays a Connected Person and in the Founder's Circle (AMKE Article 2.2). The independent proxy holder acting under Pool Constitution Article 14.3(e) under the neutral rule (Article 4.2D, Fallback) is not a Founder Party by doing so.
- **Commitments travel with the shares.** Every holder of founder-pool shares adheres, before its holding is registered (an heir, or an acquirer through seizure or bankruptcy, as Articles 5.9 and 5.9A provide), to the Pool Constitution, to the Shareholders' Agreement (Clause 12.3), Clauses 5.1B to 5.1D included, to the Brand Licence, and to these Articles (Article 5.7), and is bound by every permanence, independence and 50% rule in them, as the Founder.
- **Who may not hold them.** The company, the AE, the AMKE, the Pensionskasse, the holder of any Prozent share, a pension fund under Article 10A.10, and anyone any of them controls, may not hold founder-pool shares.
- **Oversight.** The Founder, through the Founder Representative, is at the end of every chain of supervision of the company's Agents (Article 11A.2A), except the chains that stay independent of the Founder (Articles 10A.8 and 10A.9D) and the chain for the Founding Person's own work as an Agent, which ends with the Founding Person's Supervisor at the AMKE (Article 11A.12).
- **Brand.** The Brand Assets (the nilware.io marks and domain, and the Founder's consent to the use of "nilware.io" in the company's name and distinctive title) are assets of the founder pool, held of record for it by the Founding Person until the founder pool has a legal vehicle (Pool Constitution Article 2A.3), inseparable from the Class F shares, and the company uses them under the Brand Licence (Article 1.1; Pool Constitution Article 2A). They are not founder-pool shares and do not count in the capital.

4.3 Following the Swiss practice of strengthening a company's capital base, the shareholders aim to keep paid-in capital of at least €20,000 within three years of incorporation. (This target is based on the CHF 20,000 GmbH minimum.)

4.4 Capital contributions in kind follow Law 4072/2012 Art. 77(2). Guarantee and non-capital contributions are allowed under Law 4072/2012. They must be valued, recorded in these Articles and approved by a Qualified Majority. Shares for non-capital or guarantee contributions are issued only together with the Class F, Prozent and pool shares needed to keep those percentages of the total number of shares (Articles 4.1, 4.2A and 4.2B).

## Article 5 – Share Register and Transfers

5.1 The managers keep the partners' book (βιβλίο εταίρων, Law 4072/2012 Art. 66(1)(a)), called the "share register" in these Articles. In line with Swiss transparency standards, it also lists the ultimate beneficial owners. A transfer or encumbrance takes effect towards the company and the shareholders from the written notice signed by transferor and transferee, which may be given by e-mail, and towards third parties from its entry in the partners' book (Law 4072/2012 Art. 84(1)). The managers enter it at once, once the conditions in the law and these Articles are met, and file any change with GEMI that the law requires within [20] days [GEMI filing duty: counsel note I-22].

5.1A **Beneficial owners.** The managers identify the company's beneficial owners, counting economic interests (Reserve Shares included) and control through the Founder's block vote (Article 4.2E). They register the beneficial owners, and every change, in the Central Register of Beneficial Owners under Law 4557/2018 Art. 20 within the period the law sets. Every acquirer of shares, or of a right in them, supplies its beneficial-owner information when it adheres or is registered, and again on any change.

5.2 **Right of first refusal.** A shareholder who wants to transfer shares must first offer them to the other shareholders, at the same price and terms. The offer is made pro rata to the shareholders other than the holders of founder-pool shares and the Prozent holders. This Article does not apply to founder-pool shares (Article 5.7) or to Reserve Shares (Article 4.2D, Sales and grants). The Kassenprozent pool may take part only within its band, and only where the counterparty rules in Article 10A.8 allow. The offer stays open for 30 days.

5.3 Transfers to third parties need approval by a Qualified Majority, except transfers of founder-pool shares (Article 5.7) and disposals of Reserve Shares (Article 5.6(e)). Approval may not be unreasonably withheld. Every new shareholder must first adhere to Clauses 5.1B to 5.1D of the Shareholders' Agreement.

5.4 **Tag-along.** If the majority shareholder sells control, the minority may sell its shares on the same terms.

5.4A **Change of control of the founder pool.** A person who acquires, alone or in concert with others, more than half of the Class F shares controls the Founder's block vote (Article 4.2E). Acquiring control (Article 4.2C) of a holder of founder-pool shares counts as acquiring its founder-pool shares, and shares held by persons a buyer controls count as the buyer's. The Investors may then sell their Class B shares of the AE to that person on the terms in Clause 9.1A of the Shareholders' Agreement [owner may confirm]. Prozent shares are not sold (Article 10A.5).

5.5 **Drag-along.** If shareholders holding at least 75% accept a bona fide offer for 100% of the company, the others must sell on the same terms. This does not apply to the share held for each Prozent, which stays with its holder (Article 10A.5). Pool shares are not Prozent shares and are not excluded (Article 4.2A).

5.6 **Prozent transfers.** Article 5.2 does not apply to the following transfers, nor, except where stated, Article 5.3 and the Qualified Majority for admitting new shareholders:

- **(a)** a transfer by the AE of shares equal to 1% of the capital to the holder of a new Prozent under Article 6.6;
- **(b)** purchases and sales of pool shares under Article 10A.8, which follow that Article instead of Article 5.2. A sale to a person who is not yet a shareholder still needs approval under Article 5.3;
- **(c)** a move of a Prozent's share, or of pool shares, to a successor holder under the Prozent's charter or Articles 10A.4A, 10A.9B and 10A.10;
- **(d)** a transfer by the AE to satisfy options under the option pool in the Shareholders' Agreement. The admission of an option holder who is not yet a shareholder still needs approval under Article 5.3;
- **(e)** the contribution of Reserve Shares to the AE at the Qualifying Closing, a transfer of Reserve Shares to the holder of a new Prozent, and every sale, grant or other disposal of Reserve Shares by any holder, under Article 4.2D (Conversion, New Prozente, Sales and grants). A disposal by a holder of the founder pool is also a transfer of founder-pool shares under Article 5.7.

The adherence required by the last sentence of Article 5.3 applies to every transfer listed here. The managers do not register a transferee who has not adhered, where the transfer is a voluntary transfer between living persons (Law 4072/2012 Art. 84(2)). Transfers on death, seizure and bankruptcy follow Articles 5.9 and 5.9A.

5.7 **Founder-pool shares.** Founder-pool shares are freely transferable: they may be sold, given or inherited, in whole or in part, and pledged or held in usufruct. Article 5.2, the approval in Article 5.3 and the Qualified Majority for admitting new shareholders (Article 6.5) do not apply to them, in or after the Interim Period. A transfer or encumbrance of founder-pool shares between living persons takes effect towards the company, and is registered, only if the acquirer has first adhered to the Pool Constitution, to the Shareholders' Agreement, Clauses 5.1B to 5.1D included, and to these Articles (last sentence of Article 5.3), has supplied its beneficial-owner information (Article 5.1A), and is not barred by Article 4.2E (Who may not hold them). This is a restriction under Law 4072/2012 Art. 84(2). Heirs are registered as Article 5.9 provides, and seizure and bankruptcy follow Article 5.9A. For founder-pool shares under usufruct or pledge, the vote belongs to the bare owner or the pledgor and is cast through the Founder Representative (Law 4072/2012 Art. 75(4); Article 4.2E, One block). An acquirer that is a legal person undertakes in its adherence to report any change of its control (Article 4.2C) to the managers and to the founder pool's Founder Council within [14] days, and to procure, before any change of its control closes, that its controllers and the acquirer seek the ballot under Pool Constitution Article 9.3 where it applies and make the offer in Clause 9.1A of the Shareholders' Agreement where it applies. No transfer may take the Class F shares away from exactly 50% of the capital (Article 4.2B). The internal procedures of the Pool Constitution (Pool Constitution Article 9) bind the holders among themselves, not the company.

5.8 **Exit and exclusion.** If a shareholder exits or is excluded (Law 4072/2012 Arts. 92 and 93), its shares are not cancelled but are bought, at their full value under Law 4072/2012 Art. 92(3) [valuation method: owner to fix], by the person the company designates (Law 4072/2012 Art. 92(4)): for a Prozent share or pool shares, the successor holder under the Prozent's charter or Articles 10A.4A, 10A.9B and 10A.10; for founder-pool shares, a holder of founder-pool shares or a person not barred by Article 4.2E (Who may not hold them); for Reserve Shares, a person who adheres under Article 4.2D (Sales and grants); otherwise a person the managers designate who adheres under Article 5.3. The designee adheres before the purchase is registered. The company never acquires them (Law 4072/2012 Art. 87). Under Law 4072/2012 Art. 72(6), a resolution under Law 4072/2012 Art. 93 to exclude the holder of a Prozent share needs [the consent of every other shareholder].

5.9 **Death of a shareholder.** If a shareholder dies, the heir is registered as the law requires (Law 4072/2012 Art. 85(1)), is bound by these Articles from registration, and is asked to adhere to the Shareholders' Agreement (Clause 12.3) and, for founder-pool shares, to the Pool Constitution, and to supply its beneficial-owner information (Article 5.1A). Founder-pool shares stay freely inheritable (Article 5.7). Only as a backstop for an heir who has not adhered within [three weeks] of the company learning of the death, or who is barred by Article 4.2E (Who may not hold them), the company may, within one month of learning of the death, designate under Law 4072/2012 Art. 85(2) a person who adheres to buy that heir's shares at full price, set by a valuer who is not a Founder Party [method: owner to fix]: for founder-pool shares, at the Founder Council's request, holders of founder-pool shares in proportion to their founder-pool shares (the survivors' preference), then a person not barred by Article 4.2E whom the Founder Council names [owner to confirm this backstop for founder-pool shares, or to have none]; for Reserve Shares, any person who adheres under Article 4.2D. [Whether the articles may suspend an heir's votes and distributions until adherence: counsel note I-40.]

5.9A **Seizure and bankruptcy.** If shares are seized, or their holder is declared bankrupt, the shares stay subject to these Articles, which bind every holder. The managers ask the court to order their transfer, at full price, to shareholders or to a designee who adheres (Law 4072/2012 Art. 88(1) and (2)): for founder-pool shares, at the Founder Council's request, to holders of founder-pool shares in proportion, or to a person not barred by Article 4.2E whom the Founder Council names; for other shares, as Article 5.8 provides for its designees.

5.9B **Options and transfer obligations.** Every option, return right and transfer obligation over shares under these Articles, a grant or the Shareholders' Agreement (in particular Article 4.2D, Sales and grants, and Clause 6.2A of the Shareholders' Agreement) is recorded in the partners' book (Law 4072/2012 Art. 86).

## Article 6 – Shareholders' Meeting

6.1 The ordinary meeting is held within six months of the financial year end. It approves the accounts, discharges the managers and decides on profit distribution.

6.2 Notice is given at least 20 days in advance, the Swiss GmbH standard, rather than the shorter Greek minimum. It must include the agenda and the documents.

6.3 Meetings may be held by teleconference (Law 4072/2012 Art. 71(2)). Resolutions may be taken in writing without a meeting where they are unanimous, or where every shareholder signs a document recording a majority resolution and naming the minority (Law 4072/2012 Art. 73); signatures may be replaced by an exchange of e-mail or other electronic means.

6.4 **Simple Majority.** Unless stated otherwise, resolutions pass by more than 50% of the total capital.

6.4A **Prozent votes.** The shares held for the Prozente vote as follows:

- **Pension shares.** The Pensionsprozent share and the Kassenprozent pool shares (the "pension shares") vote as the Pensionskasse board decides under Article 10A.9D, not as the AMKE's own board decides. If the board is tied, they abstain.
- **Other Prozent shares** vote as the holder's governing body decides.
- **Independence.** A Prozent share carries no vote while its holder is controlled (Article 4.2C) by a Founder Party (Article 4.2E), a manager or anyone they control, or while any of them sits on the holder's governing body or takes part in deciding how the share votes [as far as Law 4072/2012 allows]. It still counts in the total capital, and its other rights (revenue share, dividends, information) are unaffected.

6.5 **Qualified Majority.** The following need at least two-thirds of the total capital, and must include the vote of the AE. For the creation of a new Prozent, the vote of the AE is cast as Article 6.6 provides. In the Interim Period (Article 4.2D), no matter in this Article may be passed, other than the creation of a new Prozent under Article 4.2D (What the AE's rules forbid, New Prozente), the change of name under Article 1.1A, [the Founding Person's written terms as an Agent, approved as Article 11A.12 (Pay) provides: owner to confirm, counsel note I-43] and [the General Director's pay, approved as Article 8.2B provides: owner to confirm, counsel note I-47]. Removing a Prozent, lowering its contribution or changing its purpose, amending the Articles listed in Article 10A.5C in a way that clause covers, and a voluntary dissolution (Article 12.1) follow Article 10A.5.

- amending these Articles;
- increasing or reducing capital, or issuing new shares (Article 4.2B);
- mergers, splits or conversion (Article 10A.5B), and dissolution (subject to Article 12.1);
- selling all or most of the business;
- admitting new shareholders;
- related-party transactions above €[●];
- borrowing above €[●] or granting security over company assets.

6.6 **New Prozente.** A new Prozent is created by a resolution of the shareholders' meeting amending these Articles, passed by the majority Law 4072/2012 requires for amending the articles: two-thirds of the total number of shares (Law 4072/2012 Arts. 68(2)(a) and 72(5)). Articles 10A.5 and 10A.5C, as adopted in these initial Articles, already apply to every Prozent created under this Article, so the amendment adds a Prozent without introducing a new unanimity rule [whether Law 4072/2012 Art. 72(6), last sentence, nonetheless requires unanimity for it: counsel note I-1]. The Qualified Majority in Article 6.5 does not apply, and the AE's vote needs no Reserved Matters approval. The resolution may be put to the meeting only after the AE's General Meeting has approved the Prozent charter as set out below. The Prozent takes effect when the amendment is registered with GEMI. A resolution under this Article only adds a Prozent. It may not change any existing Prozent, Articles 10A.5 to 10A.5C, or the limits in this Article; any such change follows Article 10A.5 or 6.5.

- **Proposal.** The managers, or any shareholder, put forward a written Prozent charter: its name, purpose, holder, the use of its revenue share, openness and reporting rules, and how it may later move to another holder. It is a condition of the charter that the holder signs the deed of adherence under Article 5.3 before it receives the share.
- **Approval by the AE.** The AE's General Meeting approves the charter by at least two-thirds of the votes represented, all share classes voting together. The quorum is shares representing at least half of the AE's paid-up capital entitled to vote on this matter, all classes counted, present or represented, at the first and at any repeat meeting. Where Law 4548/2018 requires a higher quorum or majority, that applies (AE Article 7.5). In the Interim Period, the approval is given as Article 4.2D (New Prozente) provides.
